# Reviewed import and merge log

This log is the durable handoff between development conversations. It records
approved imports, not merely upstream commits seen by the monitor. An entry
does not imply a public release or successful device testing.

## 2026-11-08 — local beta.10 Silverfish card compatibility/layout fix

full/sealed/native receipts are in TESTING and
test-results/silverfish-card-layout-20261108. Headless native-code/service
fixtures and schematic inspection do not establish physical Bigme acceptance.

## 2026-11-08 — local dashboard card feedback and removal fix

User's Bigme report/photo: source selection saves silently, repeated taps
create many identical placements, Remove leaves its old controls visible
and BookOrbit cards are labelled Unavailable. Branch
fix/dashboard-card-feedback-20261108 starts at cd8c862b and preserves the
previously approved local Silverfish addition. No upstream import, public
push/release, device/server operation or automatic data/layout cleanup.

Own adapter-only picker feedback and checked source membership. Guard repeated
picker selection by account/section/id within the current prefix, including
renames and hidden cards; do not delete existing duplicates or change the
low-level independent-placement/source-reassignment model. Success messages
follow a successful save; failed/full/future layouts get a failure message,
without exposing raw errors. Native network confirmation remains in place.

Remove is one explicit action, deletes only its exact id/hidden/order entry,
notifies and pops the nested card editor to its rebuilt parent list. A direct
card editor rebuilds as Cards and content instead of leaving the window.
Missing entries have no active controls, stale parent rows are safe/disabled,
and a successful old remove cannot delete a new placement reusing the same id.
Menu building/radio inference/back alone never remove anything. Keep original
deferred Home refresh ownership. Native build/display tests exposed that a
general navigation-stop guard would wrongly block edits in child Display
menus; restrict that guard to Remove and retain the existing child actions.

BookOrbit built-in row cards no longer go through optional plugin registry
resolution to derive availability. Read their passive snapshot; empty but
healthy sources and usable offline caches are not Unavailable. Real failures
without cached results retain the warning. No network/provider render on
picker selection or menu paint, no books/plugin saves/sync/server writes.
Twenty SV/EN native menu/navigation cases (presentation/service fixtures)
extend the existing regression suite. TESTING records final verification and
physical limits. Components/pins/assets/bootstrap/VERSION/public beta.9 dist
and 28 helpers/407 identities/2,000 API1 paths remain unchanged.

Verification: frozen **d971b3380f3d1121275f171348dc8b051f021262**; private
ZIP **8de318f3**, 9,622,807 bytes / 28,503,866 expanded, manifest **90068e27**,

## 2026-11-08 — local Silverfish default-placement fix

User's beta.8 photos show Silverfish painted over the dock, with Reading
dashboard and Silverfish as sibling Home modules. User approves routing new
Silverfish additions into the dashboard, or onto a new ordinary Home page if
there is insufficient room, without moving existing edited placements. Base
bbbc85d9; branch fix/silverfish-default-placement-20261108. No upstream/pin/
component import, public push/release, device operation or server work.

The official frozen Silverfish v2.0.0 public Home setter always appends its
native traca_cartao to page one. Its default-on registry descriptor can also
append itself through legacy module order. OrbitUI's existing module-store
adapter now hooks the canonical registry/layout APIs: only a new first-page
placement is routed, using stable module:simpleui:traca_cartao in the bounded
dashboard when available, otherwise a new page unless matching live portrait
geometry proves ordinary Home has room. Never build or scan to estimate size.
Inactive, future or full dashboards fail closed to an ordinary new page.
Existing native placements/moves, Custom Screens, card titles/hidden/size/order
and plugin state survive unchanged. New registrations are in-memory opt-in,
without persisting a preference or changing the plugin descriptor itself.

Wrap only the identified plugin instance's public Home toggle so its getter,
repeated add, off and restart recognize the dashboard card and do not duplicate
it. Use native layout methods directly, avoiding Silverfish's legacy fallback
writer after a modern save error; restore the staged OrbitUI card config if
native save throws. This is not a filesystem transaction for partial native
store failures. No full-plugin init, screensaver takeover, pet save access,
archive modification, global require replacement, setting reset or migration.
Bootstrap/API1, 27/407 helpers/identities, 2,000-path budget, VERSION and public
dist remain fixed. TESTING records verification and the pending Bigme gate.

Frozen runtime **f60bcf98**, private ZIP **dfec7d8a**, 9,607,453 bytes /
28,451,771 expanded, manifest **b7bfc82a**, exact 2,000 installed paths. Both
full scripts/43 catalogs and all 115 sealed root files in Lua/LuaJIT pass;
18-case frozen Silverfish public API and both 23-case native input/FFI dashboard
supplements pass. Exact projected source/inventory, unchanged bootstrap/
component/pins/assets/VERSION/public dist, held-art new-blob and redacted
secret checks pass. Only the two executable adapters differ from published

bbbc85d9 beta.8 OTA receipt. No push/release, real device/plugin install or
server operation. Physical/full-plugin gate remains open. This subsequent
verification receipt changes docs only, not the frozen runtime/package.

## 2026-11-08 — local beta.7 BookOrbit/module-library menu fix

Base c820a54b, branch fix/dashboard-catalog-menus-20261108, executable eab9256f.
User reports empty sources after successful refresh, a crash when reopening
the populated picker, and a blank module library. In two OrbitUI adapters,
three numeric loop indices named `_` shadowed the translator. Initial empty
sources work, but populated labels throw; native repaint catches the error
and leaves blank chrome, while direct submenu taps can throw uncaught. The
module library throws on its first bundled entry; the same bug affects the
row/carousel chooser. Rename those three indices only, retaining data/APIs.

New test_dashboard_catalog_menus loads complete native SimpleUI settings-window
and engine code, retaining real MenuTable/ListRow/input/navigation/repaint
error handling; only display/font/service boundaries and final frame attachment
are substituted. Eight SV/EN cases reproduce failures against the frozen public
beta.7 payload and pass with the fix. They assert visible rows, no builder
warnings, refresh/dismiss/back/reopen/selection, cached reopening without HTTP,
module details and persisted display choices. The existing store suite now
calls the real bundled adapter directly and requires protected shows to finish;
its cumulative verification assertion is corrected rather than swallowed.

Verification details and physical limits: TESTING; evidence
test-results/dashboard-catalog-menus-20261108. No upstream merge/pin/component,
cache/settings reset, installer/sync policy, asset/bootstrap/API1/version or
public dist change. Private candidate validation is not a release. No push,
device/plugin installation, server call/deployment or new physical acceptance.

## 2026-11-08 — local BookOrbit shelf/smartscope dashboard cards

User approves independent BookOrbit source cards with cached-first asynchronous
refresh and only device books. Start edf54516 on
feature/bookorbit-dashboard-rows-20261108. No upstream merge, pin advancement,
push, version/release, device installation or server work authorized here.
The already available native catalog API supplies libraries/collections/
smart-scopes and source-filtered books; the server alone owns smartscope rules.
Use only exact stable BookOrbit file links in its read-only compute map API,
not title guesses or an unscoped Library Sync manifest. Confirm source visibility
before sending a filtered query; fetch all bounded pages (10,000 books/1,000
sources), reject repeats/partial responses and retain previous cache on failure.

An explicit dashboard picker adds independent placements, optionally duplicate
sources, with titles/sizes and cover row/carousel; SV/EN wording and separate
BookOrbit rows toggle retain the existing master and Want to Read policies.
Account/credential SHA256 fingerprint only, no saved credentials or request URLs.
Persist at most 32 owned source snapshots/2 MiB of path text, evicting only this
new feature's older cache rows. Disable does not delete cache. Definite missing
sources keep but suppress old membership. Snapshot paint/build does no client,
directory/manifest/map scan or filesystem sweep; only displayed covers are
existence-checked. IDs/maps/path checks run in native asynchronous subprocesses;
refuse client versions that would silently fall back to blocking HTTP.
Coalesce/serialize queued work; guard policy generation, connection fingerprint,
manager generation, mounted visible observers and suspend. Native read-only map
computation is not adopted/written back. Success TTL 15 minutes; retries back off
one minute and manual refresh bypasses TTL. Per-card subtree redraw/paging retains
other native modules and their timers. No Wi-Fi enable, book downloads, sidecar,
reading status/progress or third-party sync writes.

New v3 config fails closed on beta.6/older instead of losing new card sources;
existing v1/v2 stays unchanged until explicitly adding one. Preserve unsupported
future settings and ordinary Home/Custom layouts. One adapter path is balanced
by exact-source embedding of pure lib/bookshelf_folder_label; 27 embedded helpers,
407 canonical identities and unchanged 2,000-file API1/bootstrap limit. Original
helper bytes, component pins/assets, VERSION and public dist are unchanged.
Validation results and physical/live-server limits are recorded in TESTING.

Review covered contracts C01-C09. Notable incoming changes: Bookshelf themes,

Conflict and integration decisions:

- C01/C06: OrbitUI remains the sole plugin owner. The new SimpleUI checker must
  not auto-disable plugins or rename user patches. OrbitUI reports incompatible
  active UI plugins through its read-only guard instead. KOReader older than
  v2025.08 is rejected before either component initializes, rather than loading
  half of the combined UI. Existing preload-hook priority is unchanged.
- C02/C03/C07: preserve the Home/prose/manga dock, its gesture priority, native
  reader/profile lifecycle, page-number long-press sorting, transparent
  title/footer option, BookOrbit Cover Deck source, dynamic section labels and
  custom-action icon mapping. Bookshelf's gesture switch for the page-number
  hold is named for sorting rather than claiming it cycles styles.
- C04/C07: keep Android-safe extraction/poll policy. After SimpleUI's chrome
  change, book/stat slots now retain both mounted wrapper and module-content
  references. Stats update the content, rebuilds retarget both stats and cover
  polling, and book modules are not updated twice in one refresh. Existing
  light-background/label wrappers remain independent of upstream chrome.
- C05: Hardcover remains link/enrichment-only on the KOReader side; BookOrbit
  is still the intended progress writer. Sync-prompt sequencing and external
  BookOrbit/MAL/Patch Manager integrations were not replaced.
- C08: explicitly defer Bookshelf 5.3's automatic settings/database/cache and
  ornament moves. The path adapter keeps existing flat filenames; three
  status-line keys stay authoritative in `G_reader_settings`. Reads and writes
  therefore remain visible to earlier OrbitUI and external header patches.
  New content folders still work, and ornaments in both old/new folders are
  read without moving the user's files. The two added book-facts highlight
  columns are additive; old queries remain compatible. Do not manually run the
  upstream migration while this adapter is active.
- C09: bootstrap API 1 files, root VERSION, release tags and OTA channel are
  unchanged. New plank/shadow assets are explicitly checked in runtime archives;
  upstream README-only logos/screenshots remain excluded.

Verification: 31 Python tests, 75 OrbitUI integration cases, 343 Bookshelf suites
and all 13 SimpleUI test files pass under Lua and LuaJIT. The three existing
SQLite/slow-geometry suite skips remain, as do the two per-case native font/UI
skips. No new failure waivers. All 41 translation catalogs pass after fixing
redundant plural forms and the Lithuanian rule; the old seven hash-bound
translation exceptions were removed. Updated test doubles follow upstream API
changes, and ornament filesystem tests now support both GNU and BSD stat/touch.

The clean candidate package passes inventory checks and native libarchive/SHA

final() result forwarded to balanceRows as row_accept. The predicate uses
prefix counts, allows several busts and rejects extra/mixed ordinary pieces.

row-end slot after lead measurement allowed an unbudgeted lead decoration when
the book was placed. Keep that slot spent for this row. Update the native
wiring assertion to cover the optional fifth result rather than weakening it.

bare/oversized rows, squeeze safety, optional native defaults, balancing,

output as well as page boundaries and author adjacency. Both Lua and LuaJIT pass:

## 2026-10-17: Fix light-member box covers and bound image-picker work

User report on public alpha.42: Manga series boxes show placeholders, and
"Set series image" stays unresponsive until KOReader is force-closed.
This is a local follow-up, not another upstream import. Source pins, VERSION,
stable bootstrap/API, artwork, profiles and stored image choices are unchanged.

The slipcase hydrated a private copy of its first member for its colour/aspect,
but gave the original light member to SpineWidget. That record lacks has_cover,
so the actual cover-selection gate renders the placeholder even when a cover
exists. Reuse the hydrated, buffer-free copy for the image and subsequent
fallback resizing. Regression test fails on the original code.

Use a small shared Bookshelf image-picker helper for series/author/group,
folder and book image actions: validate the initial directory, fall back to
an existing home/cwd, use filename sorting and plain entries, and avoid
inherited book-status filtering and eager subfolder counts. Do not create an
image-library directory or rewrite settings just to open the chooser. SimpleUI
series grouping now leaves PathChooser choices alone instead of querying them
as books. The module map includes the new helper. Contracts C02/C04/C07 remain;
native navigation, long-press confirmation and existing save callbacks remain.

The device hang itself still needs confirmation after updating: no device log
was available, so eliminated synchronous work is a concrete risk correction,
not proof of the only cause of the reported lock-up. Test evidence is retained
under test-results/series-image-fix-20261017/. No publication is requested in
this turn; do not replace alpha.42's public assets.

Full sequential Lua/LuaJIT gates pass 561/562 root cases in 48 files,

## 2026-10-25: Beta 7 first-run Home fix and release preparation

The user reported a fresh Android Beta 5 installation where Home was unavailable
or KOReader crashed, persisting after a KOReader update and enabling Cover
browser. The supplied logs contain a native destroyed-mutex abort from a process
preceding the logged restart, not a matching Lua traceback. Do not claim that
this identifies or resolves the native crash. Raw device logs are not committed.
The user explicitly requested implementing the proposed fix/diagnostics and
publishing a new release immediately, after the remaining uncertainty was stated.

Confirmed independently in Beta 5/Beta 6: the built-in Home post-open hook uses
`logger.info` without importing logger, only when onboarding is absent/false.
The first-run regression fails on the previous code with a nil-global error;
configured installations skip it. Import logger explicitly. Keep the real Home
facade's shared engine identity, callbacks, host-owned onboarding and optional
cached BookOrbit refresh, rather than rewriting user flags or layouts.

The existing screen-nav module now owns Home load diagnostics and guarded open.
Home action, boot, resume, reader return and reopen use the reporting loader;
full tracebacks are logged and the real first error is shown persistently.
Only newly failed non-table entries for the two owned Home/engine modules are
restored to their previously absent values. LuaJIT's real require sentinel can
be numeric; test real require failures, not only mocked userdata. Never purge
valid tables, pre-existing state or third-party modules. Partial Home cleanup
sets intentional-close before closing, does not erase a later replacement, and
reports even if cleanup/notification fails. Closing the error message cannot
schedule an automatic Home retry. Existing navigation-generation guards remain.

Contracts C01/C02/C06/C07/C08 are preserved; no settings, links, reading data,
libraries, artwork, fonts, bootstrap/API 1 or upstream pins change. No upstream
merge is included. Four existing component files change, plus one excluded test
file; no runtime module path is added. Beta 7 uses a new version/tag/asset, never
replacement bytes for Beta 5/Beta 6. The release note consumes one additional
installed file slot under the existing API 1 limit (seven remain).

All 14 targeted cases pass under Lua/LuaJIT: fresh and configured onboarding,
actual factory/facade and action, optional host setup, callback/table identity,
load/construct/show/hook errors, bounded readable notifications/full traces,
first-cause retention, real retry sentinel cleanup and owner-safe partial close.
Final gates: both full suites, translations, tests against extracted sealed
payload, source/inventory/artwork/history/secret audits, native upgrade/rollback
from actual public Beta 5 and Beta 6, exact downloaded draft/public bytes and

## 2026-10-26: Beta 11 shared updater and safe GitHub diagnostics preparation

User sends a photo of the Bookshelf update page labelled OrbitUI, showing

cap/minimum one minute); failed checks do not poll again within that session's
deadline. Generic 403s explicitly remain unconfirmed rate limits. No bypass,
automatic retry loop, credentials or security relaxation. TLS/CA/hostname,
allowlisted redirects, byte limits and deferred activation remain unchanged.

Wi-Fi/battery/time menus as the remaining high-priority item after category

- Small anchored core ButtonDialogs, one instance at a time, share four
  capability-gated destinations, explicit Refresh and native Back/outside/Close.
  Existing SimpleUI Slider/Caption/step-control widgets retain real PowerD
  ranges, warmth conversion and readback; drag refresh stays in the slider
  region, without direct caption painting into another owner's screen buffer.
  Short screens retain native advanced light settings rather than clipped
  added widgets. Native night-mode event and advanced light dialog remain.
- Wi-Fi reads only an already-loaded core manager. Cold require could perform
  automatic restore, so even that is forbidden on opening status. No scanning,
  connect attempt, fabricated network event or optimistic connection claim.
  Android opens system Wi-Fi settings; absent toggle capability never turns
  core's generic `true` shim into a Connected label. Other supported devices
  delegate to the original core toggle prompt/network chooser on explicit tap.
- Battery/time use local status and existing enabled battery-statistics/reading-
  calendar owners. Unknown/unsupported/error values stay unknown/unavailable.
  No reading database query/provider/network work during paint or new poller.
- Status taps default OFF; existing bars/layouts/choices are untouched. A
  searchable Navigation > Quick controls page and addable in-place quick action
  expose the same UI. All owned labels/help/error messages have SV/EN text.
- Home's item key and optional measured item-widget hook preserve native
  label references/in-place refresh, spacing and long presses. Painted owner,
  custom FM corner action, navigation/modal/geometry/sleep checks guard taps.
  Bookshelf's ordinary hero and micro status use native strip geometry; custom
  token layouts are not parsed into guessed icon hit regions. A four-line
  optional seam runs only after user gestures and child controls; full-screen
  tap-to-restore, page dots and job Stop keep priority. Shared detail cards do

  a failed activity read as false for the day/window. Add a successful-empty SQL
  sentinel, validate result rows and require complete finished-ledger reconciliation
  before sealing chart totals. Original finished-book list stays the first return;
  completeness is additive. Preserve partial closed-month reuse, full historical
  invalidation and future-year retry across rollover. No new settings/sidecar writer,
  cache deletion, DB migration or raw connection replacement.
- Adopt the recursive plural parser instead of our superseded text-to-Lua
  ternary workaround (the sole textual conflict). It handles enclosing/nested
  catalogue expressions, C numeric truth, arithmetic and zero plural indices
  without executing expression strings. Preserve native gettext ownership and
  test real Slavic headers plus malformed/executable text rejection.
- Import Custom Screen Dispatcher forwarding and passive-toast Home return.
  Keep our destination/displacement and navigation-generation guards, exact
  reader/profile return, opt-in quick-control input priority and native-dialog
  ownership. No new global require/gettext hooks or user-patch auto-disable.
